Conclusion: 自旋退相干特征可作为识别碳缺陷原子结构的指纹，为光学光谱之外的结构鉴定提供了新途径。

Abstract: Carbon-related defects in hexagonal boron nitride are promising room-temperature single-spin qubits and quantum sensors, but their atomic structures remain largely unidentified. Here we show, using first-principles calculations of electron-spin decoherence, that the atomic structure of each defect is imprinted in its spin coherence. Mapping the Hahn-echo dynamics of seven candidate carbon defects across magnetic field and four isotope-engineered nuclear-spin baths, we find that electron-spin-echo envelope modulation emerges at defect-specific magnetic fields, at which the nearest-neighbor nuclear spins satisfy a cancellation condition set by their hyperfine and quadrupole couplings. Both the fields and the modulation frequencies follow from an analytical model using computed hyperfine and quadrupole tensors alone, and they shift or vanish upon isotope substitution. At low fields, the field dependence of the coherence time separates the defects into two classes according to the sublattice occupied by carbon. These decoherence fingerprints, directly testable in isotope-engineered samples, establish a structural identification route complementary to optical spectroscopy.

</details>


### [93] [Revealing Physical Redundancy in the Two-dimensional Fermi-Hubbard Model via Transferable Observable Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20836)
*Ao-Ning Wang,Min-Quan He,Z. D. Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究发现二维费米-哈伯德模型中不同可观测量（如总密度、双占据和自旋关联）之间存在显著的物理信息冗余，可通过神经网络相互重构，表明这些观测量在表征系统相图时具有高度可转移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究强关联量子体系中不同可观测量是否包含超越其表象差异的可互换物理信息。

Method: 采用基于神经网络的重构框架，在二维费米-哈伯德模型中对总密度、双占据和自旋-自旋关联三种典型可观测量进行交叉重构，并与自重构基准对比评估转移性。

Result: 发现一个可观测量的相图可以从另一个准确重构，精度接近自重构水平，尤其在平凡相区；该可转移性依赖正确的物理标记，在有限温度下持续存在，且对噪声输入保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 二维费米-哈伯德系统中不同可观测量间存在显著的物理信息冗余，为观测层面的信息冗余提供了数值证据。

Abstract: The Fermi-Hubbard model provides a paradigmatic setting for studying strongly correlated quantum matter, where different observables are commonly used to probe charge, interaction, and spin correlations. In this work, we investigate whether these observables contain mutually transferable physical information beyond their apparent distinction. We quantify such physical redundancy through transferability tests among three representative observables of the two-dimensional Fermi-Hubbard model: total density (N), double occupancy (D), and spin-spin correlation (S). Using a neural-network reconstruction framework, we find that the phase diagram of one observable can be reconstructed from another with accuracy close to self-reconstruction benchmarks, especially in trivial phase regimes. This transferability relies on correct physical labeling, persists across finite-temperature regimes, and remains robust under noisy inputs. Our results suggest that separate observables can carry a substantial fraction of one another's physical information, providing numerical evidence for observable-level redundancy in the two-dimensional Fermi-Hubbard system.

</details>


### [94] [Non-Hermitian sensing via end-to-end Green's functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20847)
*Hui-Qiang Liang,Yu-Shi Cheng,Guo-Fu Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用非厄米皮肤效应实现指数级高灵敏度且抗噪声干扰的传感方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了同时实现高灵敏度和对噪声的鲁棒性，解决传统传感方案难以兼顾这两者的挑战。

Method: 通过利用非厄米系统中端到端格林函数的指数放大（非厄米皮肤效应的直接体现），结合拓扑不变量（谱绕数）保护机制，在存在无序的情况下保持高灵敏度。

Result: 实现了灵敏度随系统尺寸L指数增长（exp(αL)），并且该性能在非厄米拓扑相保持完整时具有抗无序能力。

Conclusion: 该方案为构建兼具超高灵敏度和噪声容忍能力的传感器提供了理论基础和可行路径，尤其适用于合成非厄米平台。

Abstract: Sensing hinges on two key attributes: high sensitivity and resilience to noise. Here, we present a scheme that unites these features within a single framework. By harnessing the exponential amplification of end-to-end Green's functions-a direct signature of the non-Hermitian skin effect-our scheme achieves an exponential sensitivity scaling of exp(αL) with α a model-specific constant and L the system size. Notably, this enhanced sensitivity is protected by spectral winding numbers and endures under disorder when the non-Hermitian topological phase remains intact. We also explore its feasibility in synthetic non-Hermitian platforms, providing a pathway toward highly sensitive and noise-tolerant sensing.

</details>


### [95] [Characterizing Multimode Effects in a Guided Matterwave Gyroscope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20889)
*Jessica K. Eastman,Ellen Zheng,Stuart S. Szigeti,Simon A. Haine*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于双组分玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体的紧凑型物质波涡旋陀螺仪，利用拓扑稳定性实现与面积无关的旋转灵敏度，并通过量子模拟分析相互作用导致的性能退化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不依赖萨格纳克效应的新型陀螺仪，以实现对几何漂移鲁棒且高灵敏度的紧凑型旋转传感。

Method: 采用全量子多模态模拟方法，研究环形势中双组分玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体形成的涡旋态在相互作用下的动力学行为，并分析相扩散和四波混频等机制对其性能的影响。

Result: 发现调节相互作用和囚禁参数可在不同退化机制间产生权衡，降低组分间散射长度反而可能恶化灵敏度；相比导引萨格纳克干涉仪，该系统展现出更优的缩放性能。

Conclusion: 涡旋陀螺仪因其拓扑稳定性和优越的缩放特性，是实现紧凑高精度旋转测量的有力候选方案。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the performance of a compact matterwave vortex gyroscope formed by a two-component Bose-Einstein condensate in a toroidal potential. Unlike conventional atomic gyroscopes that rely on the Sagnac effect, the topological stability of the vortex state yields rotation sensitivity independent of the enclosed area, making the device robust against geometric drifts. Using fully quantum multimode simulations, we quantify two interaction-driven mechanisms that degrade performance: phase diffusion from one-axis-twisting and four-wave mixing from intercomponent scattering. We identify regimes where tuning interaction and trapping parameters produces a trade-off between these effects, and find that reducing the intercomponent scattering length can counterintuitively worsen sensitivity. Finally, we compare the vortex gyroscope to a guided Sagnac interferometer, demonstrating superior scaling, establishing it as a promising candidate for compact precision rotation sensing.

</details>


### [96] [Anisotropic dynamical reconstruction of quantum geometry in quenched Chern insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20895)
*Nan Li,Zhiyong Liu,Zhaoxin Liang,Ying Hu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在二维陈绝缘体的突然淬火后，量子度规发生各向异性动力学重构，其中一个本征值随时间增长，另一个几乎不变，形成与静态基态不同的动态主框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在非平衡条件下，特别是哈密顿量跨越拓扑相变时，量子几何（如量子度规）如何演化，探索其动力学行为与静态性质的区别。

Method: 通过分析突然淬火后的二维陈绝缘体系统，研究量子态在幺正动力学下的量子度规演化，关注其本征值和主轴方向的动力学变化，并结合动量空间中的相对动力学相位进行解释。

Result: 发现淬火后量子度规出现各向异性重构：一个本征值随时间增长，另一个几乎不变；主轴分别对齐于能量梯度方向和等能线切线方向，且该动态框架不同于静态基态度规；揭示了动量依赖的相对动力学相位是这一现象的起源，并导致局域和全局度规观测量具有特征性的长时行为。

Conclusion: 相干动力学可以重新组织量子几何，提出了一种非平衡机制来实现量子度规的动态控制，为调控量子几何提供了新途径。

Abstract: Under unitary dynamics, the Chern number of an evolving quantum state remains conserved even when a quench drives the Hamiltonian across a topological phase transition. In sharp contrast, we reveal an anisotropic dynamical reconstruction of the quantum metric. Following a sudden quench in a two-dimensional Chern insulator, the metric develops a principal frame in which one eigenvalue grows in time whereas the other remains nearly unchanged. At long times, the associated axes align with the energy-gradient direction and the tangent direction of the constant-energy contours of the post-quench Hamiltonian, respectively. This dynamically selected frame is distinct from that of the static post-quench ground-state metric. We identify momentum-dependent relative dynamical phases as its origin: they enhance the distinguishability of neighboring states separated along the energy-gradient direction, while states along an equal-energy contour remain nearly phase locked. Consequently, local and global metric observables acquire characteristic long-time signatures of the post-quench Hamiltonian. Our results establish a nonequilibrium mechanism by which coherent dynamics reorganizes quantum geometry, suggesting new possibilities for its dynamical control.

</details>


### [97] [Tools for Reducing Service Time in Near-Term Quantum Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20954)
*Jake Smith,Thomas R. Beauchamp,Scarlett Gauthier,Oumayma Bouchmal,Stephanie Wehner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过动态缩短纠缠生成尝试间固定间隔来回收空闲时间的方法，以优化量子网络服务时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子网络架构在纠缠生成批次间设置固定间隔，导致失败时网络利用率下降，造成资源浪费。

Method: 利用分析性执行模型，在满足硬件约束的前提下优化相邻纠缠生成尝试之间的时间间隔，动态调整以减少空闲时间。

Result: 在Arqon架构下的评估显示，单个应用的服务时间最多减少42分钟（7.6%），共调度时每个应用减少16-29分钟（26-30%）。

Conclusion: 该方法可广泛适用于共享硬件资源的量子网络架构，并可由网络调度器在线使用，有效提升网络利用率和服务效率。

Abstract: Architectures have been proposed to control entanglement generation in multi-user quantum networks. To allow time for local operations and classical communication at end nodes, these architectures insert fixed separations between consecutive batches of entanglement generation attempts. This reduces network utilization when attempts fail, leaving the network idle during the scheduled separation. To address this limitation, we propose a novel method to reclaim this idle time by shortening the scheduled separation between attempts while respecting hardware constraints. The method uses an analytical execution model to optimize the separation and reduce the total network service time of an application. Evaluations within the Arqon architecture show network service time reductions of up to 42 minutes (7.6%) for single applications and 16-29 minutes (26-30%) per application when co-scheduled. The approach applies broadly to quantum network architectures that share hardware between entanglement generation and local operations, and the method can be used online by network schedulers.

</details>


### [98] [When More Becomes Less: Topology-Reversed Three-Qubit Gate Performance on IBM Quantum Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21004)
*Simona K. Grigorova,Stancho S. Stanchev,Nikolay V. Vitanov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在IBM的156量子比特Heron处理器上，研究了线性拓扑下精确Toffoli门的不同分解方式，发现尽管$CCX_8$使用更多两量子比特门，但由于硬件连接性限制，其物理实现性能优于$CCX_6$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在受限量子硬件连接性下，如何选择最优的Toffoli门分解方案以提升量子电路的实际执行性能。

Method: 比较了$CCX_6$和$CCX_8$两种Toffoli门分解方法在线性三量子比特拓扑上的表现，使用CZ门实现并进行编译优化，通过超图态保真度和相位敏感基准测试进行评估。

Result: 实验结果显示，在大多数保留的三量子比特组合上，八CZ门的$CCX_8$实现具有更低的超图态失真率和更高的性能，优于十二CZ门的$CCX_6$实现。

Conclusion: 硬件连接性会影响精确分解方案的操作优劣排序，更多抽象两量子比特门的电路可能在实际硬件上表现更优。

Abstract: The exact Toffoli gate admits a six-CX decomposition, denoted by $CCX_6$, that is optimal under unrestricted two-qubit connectivity. On a linear three-qubit topology, however, $CCX_6$ contains 4 nearest-neighbor CX gates and 2 non-nearest-neighbor CX gates. By contrast, an alternative exact decomposition, denoted by $CCX_8$, uses only 8 nearest-neighbor CX gates. Because CCX is locally equivalent to CCZ, we perform the experiments using the corresponding $\cczs$ and $\cczl$ circuits. We compare these circuits on sampled linear triples of the 156-qubit IBM Quantum Heron processors \texttt{ibm\_fez} and \texttt{ibm\_kingston}. Under the compilation protocol, the nominal $\cczs$ circuit becomes a twelve-CZ implementation, whereas the linear-nearest-neighbor circuit retains eight native CZ gates. Experimentally measured ensemble-feature-selection estimates favor the eight-CZ realization on nearly all retained triples. We test the same ordering by preparing a three-qubit hypergraph state, which probes the coherent conditional phase rather than only computational-basis populations. The measured hypergraph-state infidelity is lower for the $\cczl$ circuit for most triples on both processors. Phase-altered interleaved randomized benchmarking provides a complementary comparison of Clifford surrogates preserving the two compiled entangling structures. Within the scope of the tested circuits and phase-sensitive input state, the results demonstrate that hardware connectivity can reverse the operational ranking of exact decompositions: a circuit with more abstract two-qubit gates can yield the better physical implementation.

</details>


### [99] [Degeneracy beyond the parity-symmetry protection in the Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21010)
*Jamil Khalouf-Rivera,Miguel Carvajal,Francisco Pérez-Bernal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了即使在宇称不守恒的情况下，量子系统中仍可存在能级双重简并的现象，其根源在于系统经典相空间中的Z2对称性导致量子系统中的反幺正Z2对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在缺乏传统对称性（如宇称）时，量子系统中简并态的产生机制。

Method: 通过引入破坏宇称但保留其他对称性的非谐项到LMG模型哈密顿量中，并分析其对基态和激发态量子相变的影响。

Result: 发现即使宇称不守恒，系统仍存在指数型能级双重简并；该现象源于经典相空间中的Z2反射对称性，对应量子系统中的反幺正Z2对称性。

Conclusion: 量子系统中能级简并不仅依赖于传统对称性，也可由经典相空间的对称性通过反幺正操作在量子层面体现。

Abstract: Degeneracy patterns in quantum mechanics stem from the system symmetries. In particular, the broken-symmetry phase in the well-known Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick (LMG) model is composed of doubly-degenerate states of different parity. In this work, we show that such doublets can exist even if parity is not conserved. For this purpose, our starting point is an anharmonic LMG Hamiltonian with a second-order ground-state quantum phase transition (GSQPT) and a rich spectrum, with two different excited-state quantum phase transitions. The inclusion in the Hamiltonian of a term inducing a first-order GSQPT breaks the parity symmetry but conserving the exponential degeneracy in the energy doublets. We demonstrate that this phenomenon can be traced back to the existence of a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry (reflection symmetry) in the system's classical limit phase space that leads to an anti-unitary $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry in the quantum system.

</details>


### [100] [Minimization of micromotion for nanoparticles in a Paul trap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21011)
*Jamie Morley,Jean Paul Louys Sansó,Dmitry Bykov,Simon Baier,Tracy Northup*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验展示了三种在直线Paul阱中沿三个轴最小化纳米粒子微运动的方法，最精确的方法可将杂散场归零至2.9 V/m以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微运动是Paul阱中带电粒子偏离交流囚禁场节点时产生的不良效应，会加热粒子、限制定位精度，并在量子实验中引起退相干，但目前尚无针对介观粒子的标准化微运动补偿程序。

Method: 实验演示了三种不同的方法来最小化线性Paul阱中纳米粒子的微运动。

Result: 最精确的方法能够将杂散场补偿到2.9 V/m以内，与已报道的离子囚禁实验中的值相当。

Conclusion: 该研究为介观粒子的微运动补偿提供了有效方案，达到了与离子实验相媲美的精度水平。

Abstract: When a charged particle in a Paul trap is displaced from the node of the AC trapping field, excess micromotion arises as an undesired effect. Excess micromotion heats the particle, limits the precision with which the particle can be localised, and acts as a decoherence channel in quantum mechanical experiments. However, thus far there is no standard procedure for micromotion compensation with mesoscopic particles. Here, we experimentally demonstrate three different methods for minimizing the micromotion of a nanoparticle in a linear Paul trap along three axes. The most precise method allows us to nullify the stray field to within 2.9 V/m, which is comparable to reported values in trapped-ion experiments.

</details>


### [101] [Quantifying Entangling Power of Controlled Unitary Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21105)
*Ankur Haldar,Pankaj Agrawal,Prasenjit Deb*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的可计算量ζ，用于量化受控酉门生成纠缠的能力，无需构造输出态即可评估任意输入下的纠缠程度，并建立了其在不同维度下的物理条件与上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在预测受控酉门生成纠缠能力时需模拟电路或对输入分布求平均，计算成本高且不够直接，因此需要一种更高效的量化工具。

Method: 引入新度量ζ，分析其与纠缠生成的关系；针对两量子比特系统确定ζ的极值对应物理条件；推广至任意维度控制与目标寄存器，推导ζ的普适上界并建立其与纯度、归一化线性熵和冯诺依曼熵等已知量的函数关系。

Result: ζ能有效判断并量化受控酉门的纠缠生成能力；得到了ζ的极值条件与饱和条件；建立了ζ与其他熵量之间的联系；在特定情况下复现了已有最优纠缠能力结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的ζ是一个实用且可计算的度量，为受控酉门的纠缠能力提供了统一、高效的分析框架，适用于任意维度的量子系统。

Abstract: Applying controlled unitary gates to generate entanglement between qubits is a routine task in both quantum communication and computation. The existing tools for predicting how much entanglement a given gate can generate require either simulation of the entangling circuit or averaging over a distribution of inputs for a given controlled unitary gate. Here, we introduce a computable quantity $ζ$ that not only determines whether a controlled unitary gate generates entanglement, but also quantifies the entanglement for any specific input without requiring the construction of the output state. For two-qubit controlled unitary gates, we establish the physical conditions corresponding to the extremum values of the proposed quantity. Extending the dimension of control and target registers to arbitrary size through a generalized controlled unitary architecture, we derive a universal upper bound on $ζ$ and identify the conditions for its saturation. Later we establish functional relation between the quantity and other known quantities, such as purity, normalized linear entropy, and von Neumann entropy. Finally, we compare our results with previous research work and show that the quantity proposed in this work achieves the previously known optimal values of entangling power of controlled unitary gates for some specific dimensions of target and control registers.

</details>


### [102] [Distributed synthesis of arbitrary graph states in quantum networks via rank-two GF(2) reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21166)
*Xiaoyi Zheng,Lin Chen,Chan-Tong Lam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的图态合成方法，利用联合Pauli-X测量与图翻转操作的数学等价性，将图态合成表述为GF(2)上差分矩阵的秩-2约简过程，显著降低了时间槽深度和资源开销，尤其在高边密度情况下优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图态合成方案随目标图的边密度增加导致时间槽深度和资源开销显著增长，亟需一种不依赖边密度的高效合成方法。

Method: 利用联合Pauli-X测量与图翻转操作的数学等价性，将图态合成转化为GF(2)上差分矩阵的秩-2约简问题，并设计基于对偶星型并发分布的物理映射及启发式算法，在Waxman物理拓扑上进行建模和蒙特卡洛实验评估。

Result: 给出了合成任意N节点图态最多只需floor(N/2)步的上界；实验表明该协议几乎在所有情况下时间槽深度更优，当边密度p≈0.3时各项资源开销均低于基线并全面超越，且目标图越密集优势越显著。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解耦了图态合成复杂度与边密度的关系，提供了一种高效、可扩展的量子网络图态合成新途径。

Abstract: Existing schemes for synthesizing graph states in quantum networks are essentially edge-by-edge constructions, so quantities such as the time-slot depth and the resource overhead grow significantly with the edge density of the target graph. This paper proposes a new method. Exploiting the mathematical equivalence between joint Pauli-X measurements and graph pivot operations, we formulate graph state synthesis as a rank-2 reduction process of a difference matrix over GF(2), and give an upper bound floor(N/2) on the number of steps for synthesizing an arbitrary N-node graph state, independent of the edge density of the target graph state. At the physical level, the joint Pauli-X measurement of each step is mapped to a dual-star concurrent distribution. We model the protocol on Waxman physical topologies with fiber attenuation and give a heuristic algorithm, and evaluate it against a strengthened Steiner baseline through Monte Carlo experiments. The experimental results show that our protocol is superior in time-slot depth almost everywhere. The entanglement resource overhead, the total number of CZ gates, and the number of Pauli measurements drop below the baseline near edge density p approximately 0.3, and are superior across the board thereafter. The denser the target graph state, the more significant the advantage.

</details>


### [103] [A Bayesian formulation of hybrid quantum-classical dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21169)
*Hilbert Kappen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯框架的扩散性量子-经典动力学方法，通过将波函数和经典变量视为随机过程的一部分，统一描述了量子与经典噪声及其关联，并将量子-经典状态估计转化为经典的隐状态推断问题，实现了滤波、平滑与标准贝叶斯时间序列推断方法的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立一个统一且自然的框架来描述量子与经典系统之间的相互作用，解决传统混合动力学中正定性、随机解缠自由度以及噪声关联处理困难的问题。

Method: 采用贝叶斯方法，构建联合概率密度P(ψ,x,t)满足经典Fokker-Planck方程，利用其二阶矩表示量子态，导出混合Lindblad方程及其随机解缠；并将量子-经典状态估计转化为基于观测轨迹的经典隐变量推断问题，结合Kushner-Stratonovich方程和粒子滤波等标准方法实现滤波与平滑。

Result: 成功导出了具有正定性和解缠自由度的混合Lindblad方程，实现了量子噪声、经典噪声及其相关性的统一建模；恢复了含相关噪声的随机主方程，并揭示了量子效应算符与贝叶斯后向消息之间的关系；数值示例表明平滑能更好重构隐藏轨迹，且完整轨迹后验分布可保留多峰性等结构特征。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯框架为量子-经典耦合系统提供了统一、灵活且与经典统计推断兼容的描述方式，连接了量子滤波、回溯与平滑与经典的前后向时间序列推断方法，为复杂混合系统建模与估计提供了新工具。

Abstract: We develop a Bayesian formulation of diffusive quantum-classical dynamics by treating the wave function and classical variables as components of an ordinary stochastic process. The joint probability density P(ψ,x,t) obeys a classical Fokker-Planck equation, while the quantum state appears as its second moment. Requiring this second moment to evolve linearly and autonomously yields the hybrid Lindblad equation and its stochastic unravelings. This construction makes positivity and unraveling freedom immediate and gives a unified description of quantum noise, classical noise, and their correlations through the covariance matrices (C,Γ,Q).
  The same stochastic representation turns quantum-classical state estimation into a classical hidden-state inference problem. Filtering and smoothing are Bayesian conditioning on the observed classical trajectory. We recover the stochastic master equation from the Kushner-Stratonovich equation with correlated noise and show how the quantum effect operator is related to the Bayesian backward message through the adjoint dynamics of the linear unraveling. The Bayesian posterior also defines a smoothed density matrix and, more generally, a posterior distribution over latent quantum-classical trajectories.
  These quantities can be approximated with standard particle filtering and smoothing methods. Numerical examples show that smoothing improves reconstruction of a hidden quantum-classical trajectory and that the full trajectory posterior can retain structure, such as multimodality, that is absent from its density-matrix second moment. The resulting framework connects quantum filtering, retrodiction, and smoothing to the standard forward-backward machinery of Bayesian time-series inference.

</details>


### [104] [The Entanglement Content of Quantum Measurement Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21185)
*Jef Pauwels,Otfried Gühne*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多粒子贝尔态测量的推广框架，引入了基于局部变换能力的测量基分类体系，并发现了四量子比特的一种新型最大纠缠基，可用于数据隐藏或蒸馏协议，同时证明并非所有形式的纠缠都能构成完整的测量基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何将两粒子系统的贝尔态测量推广到多粒子系统，是量子信息处理中亟待解决的基础问题。

Method: 提出了一个基于局部酉变换能力的测量基分类层次结构，推广了最大可纠缠态的概念，并构造了一个新的四量子比特最大纠缠测量基。

Result: 发现了一种新的四量子比特最大纠缠基，适合作为数据隐藏或纠缠蒸馏的候选；证明了并非所有类型的纠缠都能形成完整的测量基。

Conclusion: 多粒子贝尔测量可通过局部变换分类体系进行系统推广，但存在根本性限制：某些纠缠形式无法支撑整个测量基的构建。

Abstract: Bell-state measurements are essential ingredients in many protocols for quantum information processing, ranging from quantum teleportation and dense coding to entanglement distribution in quantum networks. Their power relies on the fact that they are measurements in an entangled basis of a two-particle system and that the used Bell-state basis can be generated from a single Bell state by local unitary transformations. How can these measurements be generalized to more particles? We develop a general framework for this state-to-measurement problem: We introduce a hierarchy of classes of measurement bases, distinguished by the local transformations the parties may use for their generation from a single state. This leads to a generalization of the concept of maximally entangleable (or weighted hypergraph) states and the identification of a novel maximally entangled basis of four qubits, being a candidate for data-hiding tasks or distillation protocols. Finally, we prove that not all forms of entanglement can be encoded in an entire measurement basis.

</details>


### [105] [Fixed-ray escort representations of sandwiched and $α$--$z$ Rényi divergences on von Neumann algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21214)
*Tanay Kibe,Pratik Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将α-z Rényi散度表示为普通相对熵的积分平均，利用非交换L^p空间和插值方法，在任意冯·诺依曼代数上建立了其对正常态的表示公式，并探讨了其在单次检验逆命题、强逆指数和功提取可靠性中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解α-z Rényi散度的结构及其在量子信息理论中的应用，研究其与普通相对熵之间的关系。

Method: 使用Haagerup非交换L^p空间和插值技术，将α-z Rényi散度表示为沿特定射线的固定射线escort态的相对熵的积分。

Result: 证明了对于任意von Neumann代数上的正常态，α-z Rényi散度可以表示为相对熵的积分平均；该公式在所有z>0下成立，并明确了支持条件不满足时的边界项。

Conclusion: 该表示方法不仅适用于各种参数范围，还导出了单调escort轮廓和凸序势，可用于重新表述单次检测逆命题、精确的sandwiched强逆指数以及功提取可靠性等问题。

Abstract: We represent sandwiched and $α$-$z$ Rényi divergences as averages of ordinary relative entropy. The $α$-$z$ Rényi divergence is shown to be an integral over the relative entropy of a canonical family of fixed-ray escort states along the ray $z=cα$. We prove this representation for normal states on an arbitrary von Neumann algebra, using Haagerup non-commutative $L^p$ spaces and interpolation. The formula holds for every $z>0$: for $0<α<1$ it holds when the support of the first state is contained in that of the reference state, and for $α>1$ it holds whenever the divergence is finite. When the lower-order support condition fails, we identify the exact fixed-ray support-boundary term. The representation yields a monotone escort profile and a convex order potential. We use these to reformulate one-shot testing converses, exact sandwiched strong-converse exponents, and work-extraction reliability as signed-area or level-crossing statements, and discuss a restricted two-parameter pair-conversion rate.

</details>


### [106] [Probing quantumness of superpositions of Gaussian states via Tsirelson probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21217)
*Yue Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了高斯态叠加（包括圆形态和广义圆形态）在Tsirelson预cess协议中的Tsirelson概率，发现其可违反经典界限，展现出动态非经典性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超导高斯态的非经典特性，特别是Wigner负性和亚普朗克相空间结构，并通过Tsirelson概率这一动力学判据来量化其非经典性。

Method: 对圆形态（d个相干态的叠加）解析计算Tsirelson概率，对含压缩的广义圆形态推导一般表达式，并分析参数对违反经典界限的影响。

Result: 对于d=3,5,7的圆形态，Tsirelson概率违反经典界限；广义圆形态在特定参数下可增强该违反。

Conclusion: 超导高斯态叠加是测试非经典性判据的多功能平台，其动态非经典性可通过Tsirelson概率有效揭示。

Abstract: Superpositions of Gaussian states, including circular states and generalized circular states, exhibit a rich variety of nonclassical features such as Wigner negativity and sub-Planck phase-space structures. The Tsirelson probability, central to the Tsirelson precession protocol, is defined as the average probability that a precessing quadrature yields a positive outcome when measured at equally spaced times, with the classical bound given by $1/2 \pm 1/(2d).$ In this work, we compute this probability for superpositions of Gaussian states. For circular states (superpositions of $d$ coherent states), we analytically calculate their Tsirelson probability and find violations of the classical bounds for $d=3,5,7$. For generalized circular states, which incorporate squeezing, we derive a general expression and identify parameter regimes that enhance the violation. Our results provide a comprehensive map of the dynamical nonclassicality of superpositions of Gaussian states and establish them as versatile platforms for testing nonclassicality witnesses.

</details>


### [107] [Eavesdropper-Blind Remote State Preparation and Applications to Quantum Public-Key Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21241)
*Kaniuar Bacho,Alexandru Cojocaru*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种弱化的远程态制备形式——窃听者盲远程态制备（EB-RSP），仅要求对协议外部观察者保持盲性，并基于单向群作用构建了两轮EB-RSP协议，为不依赖陷门假设的RSP类原语提供了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的远程态制备（RSP）均依赖于强密码学假设（如陷门爪形函数），限制了其在更广泛场景下的应用。本文旨在探索在更弱安全模型下仍适用于实际密码协议的RSP变体，以降低对复杂假设的依赖。

Method: 引入窃听者盲远程态制备（EB-RSP）概念，仅要求对外部窃听者实现盲性；基于特定的单向群作用构造两轮EB-RSP协议，并验证其安全性；同时分析现有TCF-based RSP方案是否自然适用于EB-RSP模型。

Result: 提出了EB-RSP定义并证明其足以支持量子公钥加密等应用；基于单向群作用实现了不依赖陷门的两轮EB-RSP协议；展示了传统TCF-based RSP可适配于EB-RSP框架。

Conclusion: EB-RSP是一种有意义的弱化RSP模型，可在较弱安全假设下实现重要密码功能，为构建轻量级量子-经典交互协议开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Remote state preparation (RSP) is a central primitive in quantum cryptography, enabling classical parties to remotely construct quantum states using only classical communication. As a result, RSP serves as a key building block in numerous protocols involving classical clients and quantum servers, allowing classical parties to leverage the advantages offered by powerful quantum computers. All known constructions of RSP rely on strong cryptographic assumptions, typically variants of trapdoor claw-free functions (TCFs).
  In this work, we initiate the study of a weaker form of remote state preparation, which we call eavesdropper-blind remote state preparation (EB-RSP). Informally, EB-RSP requires blindness only against external observers who see the transcript of the honest protocol, rather than against the quantum server itself. Despite this relaxed adversarial model, the resulting notion remains sufficient for useful cryptographic applications. In particular, we show that two-message EB-RSP already suffices to construct quantum public-key encryption with classical public keys and quantum ciphertexts. We then construct two-message EB-RSP protocols from specific one-way group actions, yielding a first step toward RSP-type primitives based on assumptions that do not rely on trapdoors. Finally, we observe that existing RSP constructions are likely naturally adaptable to the two-message EB-RSP notion; we demonstrate this explicitly for a concrete TCF-based RSP construction.

</details>


### [108] [Tomographic Limits of the Petz Recovery Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21309)
*Peter Sidajaya,Clive Cenxin Aw,Mingxuan Liu,Valerio Scarani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Petz恢复映射在量子态层析中的应用，指出直接迭代Petz方法的局限性，并提出通过扩展Petz构造来恢复贝叶斯和最大似然层析结构的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Petz恢复映射作为量子贝叶斯推理类比在量子态层析中应用的可能性。

Method: 讨论迭代Petz恢复的最新结果，并将其与量子态层析的贝叶斯方法联系起来，提出扩展Petz构造的方法。

Result: 发现直接迭代Petz存在局限性，而扩展Petz构造能够恢复贝叶斯和最大似然层析的结构。

Conclusion: 扩展的Petz构造为实现量子态层析提供了新的视角，揭示了进行量子反向预测所需的具体修改或细微差别。

Abstract: The Petz recovery map is considered one of the key candidates for the quantum analogue of Bayesian inference and Jeffrey's conditionalization. Since, there seems to be a natural connection between Bayesian inference and the notion of state tomography, it is natural to ask if the Petz recovery can be used for this latter task. In this paper, we discuss such recent results on iterated Petz recovery and relate them to Bayesian approaches to quantum state tomography. We highlight the limitations of direct Petz iteration and show how an extended Petz construction, by lifting the inference problem to a classical distribution over candidate quantum states, recovers the structure of Bayesian and maximum likelihood tomography. This provides perspective on the modifications or nuances required for a Petz approach to quantum retrodiction to perform quantum state tomography.

</details>


### [109] [Hypothesis testing between quantum ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21321)
*Jian Yao,Quntao Zhuang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限量子系综的二元假设检验，推导出误差概率的基本极限，并揭示了在多样本极限下，判别性能由样本数以内的完整矩层次决定，应用结果于光学通信和$t$-设计中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系综在量子信息处理中具有重要作用，但其与经典-量子态的区别以及判别时的误差极限尚不明确，因此需要建立针对有限量子系综的假设检验理论。

Method: 通过引入伴随经典标签的采样态，将观测到的标签模式与加权系综矩关联，构建贝叶斯最优测量，推导有限样本下的精确误差及大样本下的Chernoff界与误差指数。

Result: 得到了有限纯态系综在多样本下的精确误差指数；对于大的$t$，有限均匀纯态$t$-设计的最大判别指数按$\sim t^{-2}$缩放，而固定误差检测所需样本量按$\sim t^2$增长。

Conclusion: 量子系综的判别性能由其完整的矩层次结构决定，该框架为理解量子系统中的信息提取提供了基本工具，并在光学通信和量子设计中有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Quantum state ensembles are important in quantum information processing. For example, quantum $t$-designs model highly entangled states in complex systems, while projected ensembles appear in generative quantum machine learning and studies of thermalization. With their sample state accompanied by a classical label, these ensembles contain operational information beyond their average density operators. Yet an ensemble differs from a classical-quantum state because it is invariant under permutations of labels. We formulate binary hypothesis testing between finite quantum ensembles and derive fundamental limits on error probability. Given an observed label pattern, we show that the joint sampled state can be described by power-weighted ensemble moments. This yields the Bayes-optimal measurement and exact finite-sample error, revealing that discrimination is governed by the full moment hierarchy up to the number of samples. In the many-sample limit, we derive Chernoff bounds and obtain exact error exponents for finite uniform pure-state ensembles. We apply these results to optical communication and $t$-designs. For finite uniform pure-state $t$-designs with large $t$, the maximal discrimination exponent scales sharply as $\sim t^{-2}$, while equal-prior fixed-error testing requires $\sim t^2$ samples.

</details>


### [110] [Quantum annealing through a first-order phase transition: field theory approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21329)
*Diego Andrés Rivera Orona,Predrag Punoševac,Nikolai A. Sinitsyn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于描述量子退火计算中缺陷动力学的场论，预测了在一级相变过程中误差生成率的幂律行为，并指出连续参数变化下幂指数的急剧变化可作为一级临界点的标志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一级相变中系统陷入亚稳态导致大量激发的问题，旨在理解并减少量子退火计算中的缺陷生成。

Method: 基于场论方法分析量子退火过程中的缺陷动力学，利用驱动型Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick模型（LMGm）作为展示该行为的最小模型。

Result: 理论预测了在穿过或接近一级相变时，误差生成率遵循多个幂律关系，且幂指数随参数连续变化而发生突变。

Conclusion: 观测到的这种行为可作为识别一级临界点的标志，有助于避免这些点以提升量子计算性能。

Abstract: Unlike second-order phase transitions, a first-order transition has a stage, in which a system is trapped in a metastable state. The decay of this state leads to abundant excitations over the ground state. We present a field theory for kinetics of defects emerging during quantum annealing computations. This theory predicts several power laws for the error generation rate during quantum annealing either though or near the first-order phase transition. Sharp changes between the power exponents are predicted for continuous parameter changes. Observation of this behavior would be a signature of first-order critical points and could help identify and avoid them for better computations. The driven Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick model (LMGm) serves as the minimal model of interacting Ising spins that demonstrates this behavior.

</details>


### [111] [Slepian Bounds on the Success Probability of Virtual Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21342)
*Masayuki Ohzeki,Sasuke Kimata,Xinwei Lee,Hoong Chuin Lau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出虚拟蒸馏作为一种近似误差缓解方法，本质上是一种频谱滤波，无法在测量结果集中产生新的浓度，其成功概率受限于Slepian算子的主特征值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究虚拟蒸馏在有限带变分态下的局限性，揭示其作为频谱滤波的本质及其对测量结果集中度的影响。

Method: 通过构建基于变分带和结果窗口的Slepian集中算子，分析虚拟蒸馏的滤波特性，并在布尔超立方体上实现具有Krawtchouk核的Walsh带模型。

Result: 证明了虚拟蒸馏不能在指定结果集内创造新的浓度，成功概率受Slepian算子主特征值限制，且高成功率谱成分数量受限于Slepian有效维度；在2-正则Max-Cut的含噪QAOA计算中验证了带内提升与带外失效现象。

Conclusion: 虚拟蒸馏的效果受限于原始密度矩阵中的主导特征成分，其性能边界可通过Slepian理论进行刻画，为误差缓解策略的设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Virtual distillation is a powerful near-term error-mitigation primitive, but it is also a spectral filter: it amplifies the dominant eigenvector component already present in the noisy density matrix. We show that, for a finite-band variational state, this filtering cannot create new concentration inside a set of accepted measurement outcomes. The asymptotic success probability after distillation is bounded by the leading eigenvalue of a Slepian concentration operator built from a specified variational band and that outcome window. Moreover, the number of robust high-success spectral components is limited by the Slepian active dimension. \rev{For bit-string outcome windows, an explicit Walsh-band realization has a Krawtchouk kernel on the Boolean hypercube. Finite-size noisy-QAOA calculations for 2-regular Max-Cut illustrate both the in-band improvement and the out-of-band failure modes predicted by the branch-resolved result.

</details>


### [112] [Classical versus non-classical photon states for detecting vacuum non-linearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21361)
*N. Ahmadiniaz,C. Kohlfürst,R. Shaisultanov,R. Schützhold*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过在量子电动力学非线性真空探测方案中引入经典或非经典光子态，可显著提高信号的可检测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了验证量子电动力学（QED）关于真空具有非线性介质特性的预测，传统方案依赖于探测原本应为空的模式中的光子信号。本文旨在探索如何提升此类实验的探测灵敏度。

Method: 研究在原本为真空的模式中输入经典光子态或非经典态（如压缩态）对信号探测的影响，并分析其在QED真空非线性效应检测中的增强作用。

Result: 发现使用经典或非经典态替代初始真空态可以显著提高信号的可检测性。

Conclusion: 改进后的方案通过引入非真空初态有效增强了非线性真空效应的探测能力，为未来实验提供了更优路径。

Abstract: Quantum electrodynamics (QED) predicts that the vacuum should behave as a non-linear medium. For many schemes aimed at verifying this fundamental prediction, the signal consists of one or more photons in a certain mode (determined by polarization $σ$ and wave-number $\boldsymbol{k}$) which would be empty in the absence of a QED vacuum non-linearity. Here we consider modifying these schemes by sending in a classical or non-classical (e.g., squeezed) photon state instead of the initial vacuum state in that mode. We find that the detectability can be improved significantly.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Resolving positive semi-definiteness in physics-informed kernels for scientific machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20985)
*J. Moser,C. Albert,S. Ranftl*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个简单且充分的条件，确保在使用线性微分算子时核函数保持正半定性，从而为算子引导的核方法提供了统一的有效性基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学机器学习中，微分算子被用于将物理结构嵌入模型，但尚不清楚这些构造在何种条件下能保持正半定性，以及不稳定性是源于建模问题还是数值误差。

Method: 通过理论分析，提出对于m阶线性微分算子，只要基核函数m次连续可导，即可保证正半定性。

Result: 该条件适用于具有非常数甚至不连续系数的算子，涵盖扩散、弹性、波传播和量子系统等广泛物理问题。

Conclusion: 观察到的不稳定性主要源于数值问题而非建模错误，为算子引导的核方法提供了统一的合法性基础。

Abstract: Many modern machine learning models can be understood as kernel-based function-space models, including Gaussian processes and neural tangent kernels. In scientific machine learning, differential operators are increasingly used to encode physical structure directly into such models. However, it has remained unclear under which conditions these constructions are valid machine learning models, i.e. preserve positive semi-definiteness, and whether observed instabilities arise from ill-posed modeling or numerical effects. Here, we establish a simple and sufficient condition for positive semi-definiteness: for linear differential operators of order m, the base kernel must be m-times continuously differentiable. Crucially, this guarantee holds for operators with non-constant and even discontinuous coefficients. Examples are ubiquitous in physical systems, including diffusion, material elasticity, wave propagation in inhomogeneous media, and quantum systems. We conclude that remaining instabilities are attributable to numerical issues, providing a unifying validity foundation for operator-informed kernel methods.

</details>

### [114] [Calibrating Inelastic Markets to Options: The Lean Marketron and the Generalized Langevin Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20589)
*Andrey Itkin*

Main category: q-fin.CP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对Marketron模型的简化方法，通过消除对称性和固定非金融参数，将十八参数模型缩减为九参数的稳健核心模型，并揭示了价格动态中的记忆效应与市场流动风险的价格形成机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始的Marketron模型存在结构不可识别性问题，导致优化困难且经济变量无法准确测量，本文旨在构建一个更稳健、可识别的简化模型。

Method: 通过去除尺度和符号对称性、依据明确标准冻结非金融参数、绝热消除快速隐藏信号，并结合流动物理测度到风险中性测度的分阶段校准，构建了一个九参数的简化模型；利用高斯-牛顿Hessian矩阵零空间分析和流形边界分析验证模型可识别性。

Result: 得到了一个无精确对称性、不可进一步约简的九参数核心模型；发现流动与回报创新之间的扩散相关性可解释短期偏度；首次识别出流动风险的市场价格；揭示股价对数遵循带有闭式状态调制记忆核的广义朗之万方程，且记忆变量是该核的精确马尔可夫提升；提出了信号与记忆松弛速率相等的可检验条件，在SPX数据上两者分离，表明市场处于受驱动的非平衡态。

Conclusion: 简化后的Marketron模型解决了原模型的结构性缺陷，提供了对流动风险定价和市场记忆效应的清晰解释，并将主动物质类比转化为可证伪的约束条件，增强了模型的实证价值和理论意义。

Abstract: The Marketron model of \cite{HalperinItkin2025Mark} and its option pricing extension in \cite{HalperinItkinMarketron2} suffer from structural non-identifiability: an eighteen-parameter space traps solvers in suboptimal local minima and renders economic quantities unmeasurable. By removing exact scaling gauges and sign symmetries, freezing non-financial parameters by explicit criteria, and adiabatically eliminating the fast hidden signal, we derive a robust nine-parameter reduced model. A Gauss-Newton Hessian with empty null space and a manifold-boundary analysis confirm that the reduced core carries no exact symmetry and admits no further reduction. A diffusive correlation between flow and return innovations captures the short-maturity skew. A staged calibration from the physical measure to the risk-neutral measure, illustrated on SPX options, fits the whole surface with a single parameter set. The same reduction turns the wedge between the physical and pricing values of the flow block into a well-defined market price of flow risk rather than a ridge artifact, identifiable here for the first time, though a single surface constrains its level only weakly. Finally, our analysis reveals that in the Marketron model the log-price obeys a generalized Langevin equation with a closed-form, state-modulated memory kernel, and that the memory variable itself is the exact Markovian lift of this kernel. This mapping also yields a testable condition, the equality of the signal and memory relaxation rates, which on the SPX surface come out well separated, though both weakly identified, placing the fitted market tentatively in the driven, non-equilibrium regime and turning the active-matter reading from an analogy into a falsifiable constraint.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Criticality in Neural Network Function Space through Wilsonian Fixed Points and Finite-Width Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20403)
*Eric Howard,Iftekher S. Chowdhury,Hardique Dasore,Hom Nath Dhungana*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出了神经网络-量子场论对应中的威尔逊临界性解释，将无限宽度高斯过程极限视为自由场不动点，有限宽度修正作为产生非高斯相互作用的扰动，揭示了有限宽度效应在神经网络函数空间中引入相互作用、复杂性和相变行为的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索神经网络在函数空间中的临界行为，理解有限宽度对表达能力、复杂性和训练动态的影响，建立神经网络与量子场论之间的理论联系。

Method: 采用威尔逊重整化群视角，将神经网络函数分布建模为有效作用量，分析宽度、深度、激活函数、初始化方差等参数对系统临界性的影响，研究有限宽度算符的相关函数及其标度行为。

Result: 发现有限宽度效应是导致神经网络进入临界状态的核心机制；当高阶关联函数显著且有限宽度算符成为相关或临界标度因子时，函数分布对尺度结构敏感；过参数化被理解为抑制交互项之间关系的过程。

Conclusion: 该框架为通过威尔逊不动点、扰动和临界面研究神经网络的可训练性、泛化能力和架构普适性提供了理论基础，强调有限宽度在形成临界结构中的核心角色。

Abstract: Neural networks can be studied not only as parameterized computational models but also as probability distributions over functions. In this paper we develop a Wilsonian interpretation of criticality in the neural network-quantum field theory correspondence, treating the infinite width Gaussian-process limit as a free field fixed point and finite width corrections as perturbations that create non-Gaussian interactions. In this way, we do not see the departure from infinite width as a small approximation error but as the process by which interaction, complexity, expressivity and phase-like behaviour enter neural network function space. Width, depth, activation nonlinearity, initialization variance, and training dynamics are considered as control parameters that change the effective action of the network ensemble. The critical regime is when the higher-order connected correlation functions become non-negligible, and when the finite width operators become relevant or marginal scaling factors, and when the function distribution becomes sensitive to scale-dependent structure. There we can view overparameterization as suppressing a relationship of interacting terms and we find that the critical structure of finite neural networks is given by finite-width effects. This framework is a theoretical basis for studying trainability, generalization, and architectural universality of neural network functions through Wilsonian fixed points, perturbations, and critical surfaces.

</details>


### [116] [Graphon Spin Systems as Exactly Solvable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20410)
*Artem Alexandrov,Georgi S. Medvedev*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文利用图函数（graphons）研究收敛图序列上的伊辛模型，推导了其平均场极限并得到了相变的精确结果，表明临界温度由图极限对应的希尔伯特-施密特算子的特征值决定，并在 Erdős-Rényi、小世界和幂律网络等典型随机网络模型中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受组合数学和图论问题启发，图函数被用于描述复杂网络上动力学过程；本文旨在将伊辛模型推广到一般图序列上，以统一分析不同网络拓扑中的相变行为。

Method: 采用图函数作为极限对象建模收敛图序列，结合平均场理论推导自由能泛函，通过分析相关希尔伯特-施密特算子的谱性质确定临界温度，并用蒙特卡洛模拟验证多稳态现象。

Result: 得到了伊辛模型在图函数上的平均场极限和相变条件，明确临界温度与图函数对应算子特征值的关系；在小世界网络中发现了铁磁、反铁磁相及自由能局部极小值共存导致的多稳态现象。

Conclusion: 图函数框架下的伊辛模型兼具解析可解性和对多种网络结构的适应性，为复杂网络上的统计物理系统研究提供了新视角和工具。

Abstract: Graphons are measurable functions used to describe the asymptotic behavior of convergent graph families. Originally motivated by problems in combinatorics and graph theory, graphons have found numerous applications in the modeling and analysis of dynamical processes on networks.
  In this work, we use graphons to formulate the Ising model on convergent graph sequences, which include many network topologies common in applications. We derive the mean-field limit for the resulting model and obtain exact results for phase transitions in such systems. Specifically, we show that the critical temperatures of the Ising model on graphons are determined by the eigenvalues of the Hilbert-Schmidt operator associated with the graph limit. For many important network topologies, these eigenvalues can be computed explicitly.
  We illustrate our results with three representative random network models: Erdős-Rényi, small-world, and power-law. In the small-world case, we demonstrate phase transitions to both ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic phases, as well as coexistence of local minima of the free energy. The latter gives rise to multistability, as confirmed by Monte Carlo simulations.
  The results of this work demonstrate that the Ising model on graphons combines the analytical tractability of exactly solvable mean-field models with the ability to accommodate a broad range of network topologies. We expect that the use of graphons in spin models will lead to new insights into the statistical physics of interacting systems on complex networks.

</details>


### [117] [The Trachenko-Zaccone equation: nonlinear relaxation from glasses to complex systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20917)
*Alessio Zaccone,Valeriy V. Ginzburg,Oleg V. Gendelman,Sofia F. Mauro,John C. Mauro*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文综述了Trachenko-Zaccone方程的概念发展，强调其在无序凝聚态物质中非Debye弛豫的非线性动力学框架中的作用，并探讨其在复杂系统中的广泛应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在追溯Trachenko-Zaccone方程的发展历程，阐明其物理起源、数学结构及可能的推广形式，展示其在多领域中的适用性。

Method: 通过回顾该方程在应力弛豫、玻璃材料、聚合物弛豫和种群动力学中的应用，结合作者与Kostya Trachenko的合作经历，分析其理论基础与扩展方向。

Result: 揭示了Trachenko-Zaccone方程不仅适用于玻璃弛豫，还可作为描述复杂系统中非线性反馈现象的通用模型。

Conclusion: Trachenko-Zaccone方程应被视为一种具有广泛适用性的非线性反馈方程，未来可在时空分辨、网络化和随机化等方面进一步拓展。

Abstract: The Trachenko-Zaccone equation provides a compact nonlinear dynamical framework for describing non-Debye relaxation in disordered condensed matter. Originally developed to rationalize stretched- and compressed-exponential relaxation in liquids and glasses from the dynamics of interacting local relaxation events, the same equation has subsequently appeared in broader contexts, including polymer relaxation and nonlinear models of global population dynamics. This review retraces the conceptual development of the equation, with particular emphasis on its physical origin in Kostya Trachenko's feed-forward interaction mechanism, its mathematical structure, and its possible generalizations. We also include personal recollections of the work with Kostya Trachenko at Queen Mary University of London in August 2019, during which the equation emerged in essentially its present form. After reviewing applications to stress relaxation, glassy materials, polymer relaxation and population dynamics, we discuss future directions including time-dependent feedback parameters, coupled order parameters, heterogeneous and spatially resolved formulations, flux terms, network versions and stochastic extensions. The central theme of the review is that the Trachenko-Zaccone equation should be viewed not only as a model of glassy relaxation, but as a general nonlinear feedback equation with potential applications across complex systems.

</details>


### [118] [Conservation capacity of local learning rules in physical networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20426)
*Bijaya Dangol*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种关于局部学习规则保护记忆容量的理论，揭示了电路拓扑结构如何通过守恒质量的数量决定记忆容量，并证明每个输入分离的电路扇区具有独立的守恒质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决物理学习规则中守恒质量一般形式的开放性问题，探究局部测量训练电阻网络时记忆容量的限制因素。

Method: 利用基尔霍夫定律和输入边界条件，结合特勒根定理的局域化分析，对任意非线性支路定律下的电路进行拓扑分析，并通过大规模线性电路的精确计算验证守恒量的闭式表达。

Result: 发现电路被输入电极分割的每个扇区都拥有独立的守恒质量，其数量由拓扑决定且不超过输出电极数；输出钳位的学习方式会以平方乘子速率耗散对应扇区的质量；在线性电路中获得了守恒恒等式的闭式计数公式。

Conclusion: 记忆容量由电路拓扑决定，每个扇区可承载一个设计质量，差异项由拓扑贡献，额外保护泛函需额外标量广播，从而建立了一个基于电路结构的记忆容量理论。

Abstract: How many memories can a local learning rule protect, and what fixes the number? Physical learning rules train resistive networks through local measurements, and the standard rules conserve a mass-like function of the conductances, a law whose general form was posed as an open problem with the expectation that no useful solution theory exists. We answer it, in the direction the expectation ran against, and the answer is a capacity theory. The Tellegen identity behind the conserved mass localizes: the feedback state is pinned to zero at the inputs, so every sector of the circuit that the input electrodes separate carries its own private conserved mass, by an argument consuming only Kirchhoff's law and that boundary condition, hence valid for arbitrary nonlinear branch laws. The number of independent sector masses is a topological property of the circuit, bounded by the number of output electrodes. An untrainable element can destroy the mass of its own sector and of no other, which says where fixed nonlinear components may be placed; per-edge learning rates select which functionals are conserved and never how many; and adjoint coupled learning, which clamps its outputs before measuring, drains every output-carrying sector at a rate set by the squared output multipliers. In linear circuits we then classify the identities: exact rational computation over all 502 circuits on up to five vertices with two inputs and one or two outputs, and hundreds of larger ones, yields a closed-form count, proved on that family and conjectured in general, with the sector statement a theorem at every circuit size. The anticipated series-parallel polynomial laws appear as exactly the series-class cubic differences, and the number the opening question asks for is a budget: one designed mass per sector, the differences the topology donates, and one broadcast scalar for each protected functional beyond.

</details>


### [119] [Aging Phase Diagram and Exact Asymptotic Energies of Mixed Spherical Spin Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20623)
*Johannes Lang,Vincenzo Citro,Luca Leuzzi,Federico Ricci-Tersenghi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究了混合球形(p+s)-自旋玻璃在淬火到零温时的老化相图，发现了一、二及连续多个有效温度之间的转变，并得到了渐近动力学能量的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索混合球形自旋玻璃系统在极端条件下的老化行为及其相变特性。

Method: 通过求解Cugliandolo和Kurchan的渐近动力学方程，并结合数值积分验证理论预测。

Result: 发现了具有一个、两个及连续多个有效温度的老化态之间的相变，存在离散与连续层级共存的相；对于包含两体及高阶相互作用的模型，梯度下降可达到算法能量下限；而仅有高阶相互作用时则高于该下限。

Conclusion: 不同类型的相互作用显著影响系统的最终老化状态和能量弛豫行为，分离较远的相互作用阶数有助于实现更低的能量状态。

Abstract: We determine the aging phase diagram of mixed spherical $(p+s)$-spin glasses quenched from random configurations to zero temperature. Solving the asymptotic dynamical equations of Cugliandolo and Kurchan, we find transitions between aging states with one, two, and continuously many effective temperatures, including phases in which discrete and continuous hierarchies coexist. We obtain analytic expressions for the energies reached asymptotically by the dynamics. In models combining pairwise and higher-order interactions, and sufficiently separated interaction orders ($s>3$), a fully continuous phase exists, where the gradient descent relaxation converges to the algorithmic energy lower bound. Instead, in mixed models with only high-order interactions ($p\ge3$) the energy relaxation remains strictly above it. Numerical integration of the dynamical equations is consistent with the predicted two-step and mixed discrete--continuous aging states.

</details>


### [120] [Maxwell's Demon in Markov Chain Monte Carlo: Cooling Information Flow and Entropy Balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21337)
*Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文将马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛算法中的接受模块比作麦克斯韦妖，通过测量提议边并利用接受/拒绝比特来调控概率流，揭示了决策比特中包含的香农互信息及其在非可逆链中冷却信息流与维持循环的分离关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨MCMC算法中接受机制的信息处理本质，借鉴麦克斯韦妖概念理解其如何通过信息获取和反馈控制实现分布采样。

Method: 采用信息论方法分析接受模块的行为，将接受/拒绝决策视为信息存储与利用过程，并推导相对熵松弛率的分解公式。

Result: 发现决策比特携带关于提议的真实香农互信息，其中仅有方向性部分转化为冷却信息流；提出了v(t)=\dot{\mathcal I}_{m cool}(t)+\dot S(t)的关系式，实现了有用冷却与维持循环的分离。

Conclusion: MCMC的接受机制可类比为麦克斯韦妖，其信息处理过程能有效区分对系统收敛有贡献的冷却信息流和无贡献的维持循环，在非可逆链中提供了理解采样效率的新视角。

Abstract: Markov chain Monte Carlo algorithms can be viewed as feedback devices that compare a proposed move with the target distribution and then accept or reject it. In this paper the Maxwell demon is identified with the acceptance module: it measures a proposed edge, stores the outcome in the accept/reject bit, and uses that bit to shape the probability current. The decision bit carries a genuine Shannon mutual information about the proposal, whereas only its directional part is converted into a cooling information flow. The relative-entropy relaxation rate obeys $v(t)=\dot{\mathcal I}_{\rm cool}(t)+\dot S(t)$, which separates useful cooling from housekeeping circulation in nonreversible chains.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [A Hybrid Edge Cloud Digital Twin for Welfare-Constrained Control in Poultry Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20367)
*Suresh Neethirajan*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种边缘-云数字孪生框架，用于家禽养殖环境的实时、动物福利约束下的气候控制，结合物理模型与数据驱动方法，在实际农场条件下显著提升了控制精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有家禽养殖气候控制多依赖经验性规则，难以保障动物福利和运行效率，缺乏对生物与环境动态耦合的精确建模与实时调控能力。

Method: 构建了一个边缘-云协同的数字孪生系统，融合分布式传感、设备端状态估计、混合物理-数据模型（灰箱热力学与质量平衡模型+学习残差项）以及模型预测控制，支持带宽受限环境下的异步同步与跨场学习。

Result: 在高保真肉鸡养殖测试平台中，温度预测误差从1.8°C降至0.4°C，氨气约束违规减少90%，通信需求降低约30倍，域迁移评分为0.92，显示良好跨设施鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于物理的数字孪生结合实时控制可实现可扩展、兼顾动物福利的生物生产系统管理，为智能农业提供可行路径。

Abstract: Poultry production operates under tightly coupled environmental and biological dynamics, yet commercial climate control remains largely heuristic, limiting welfare assurance and operational efficiency. We introduce an edge-cloud digital twin framework for real-time, welfare-constrained environmental control in poultry facilities. The framework integrates distributed sensing, on-device state estimation, a hybrid physics-data model, and model predictive control to enable anticipatory and adaptive management under practical farm constraints. A grey-box thermodynamic and mass-balance formulation is augmented with a learned residual that captures unmodeled biological variability, including activity-dependent metabolic heat. This hybrid model is embedded within a state-space representation for real-time estimation and control at the edge, while cloud coordination supports cross-farm learning and long-horizon optimization. Bandwidth-aware processing and asynchronous synchronization enable deployment in connectivity-limited environments. Evaluation in a high-fidelity broiler production testbed demonstrates substantial gains over rule-based control and physics-only modeling. Temperature prediction error is reduced from 1.8 degrees Celsius to 0.4 degrees Celsius, ammonia constraint violations decrease by 90 percent, and communication requirements are lowered approximately 30-fold through edge-first processing. A Domain Transfer Score of 0.92 further indicates strong robustness across facility conditions. These results show that physically grounded digital twins, coupled with real-time control, enable scalable and welfare-aware management of biological production systems.

</details>


### [122] [Infrared Hotspot-Guided Early Warning of Lithium-Ion Battery Thermal Runaway Under Mechanical Abuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20383)
*Syed Sajid Ullah,Salman Khan,Muhammad Zunair Zamir*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段早期预警方法，通过红外热点动力学评估局部热不稳定性，并融合多模态特征实现锂电池机械滥用下的早期预警，显著提前了热失控检测时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械滥用可能在传感器信号显著变化前就引发锂离子电池的热失控，因此需要更早、更可靠的预警机制。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：第一阶段基于红外热点动态估计局部热不稳定性的得分；第二阶段将该得分与机械、电气、热和图像强度特征融合，进行综合预警。使用实验内三折交叉验证，并采用out-of-fold策略避免模型乐观偏差。

Result: 仅热点动力学在第一阶段达到ROC-AUC 0.945，两阶段分类器在第二阶段达到ROC-AUC 0.908，优于直接多模态融合方法。热梯度上升比电压检测平均提前40帧（4秒），在固定阈值0.5下平均领先14.8帧。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段方法不仅提高了早期预警性能，还保留了可解释的中间不稳定性信号，有助于电池管理系统更早干预，提升安全性。

Abstract: Mechanical abuse can trigger thermal runaway (TR) in lithium-ion batteries through localized heat generation before sensor signals become decisive. This paper proposes a two-stage early-warning approach that estimates localized thermal instability from infrared hotspot dynamics and then fuses this instability score with mechanical, electrical, thermal, and image-intensity features for a 20-frame warning horizon. Evaluation uses repeated experiment-wise three-fold validation, with out-of-fold Stage-I scores during Stage-II training to prevent stacked-model optimism. Hotspot dynamics alone achieve Stage-I ROC-AUC 0.945, and the two-stage classifier reaches Stage-II ROC-AUC 0.908, exceeding direct multimodal fusion while preserving an interpretable intermediate instability signal. Thermal gradient rise precedes voltage-based detection by 40 frames (4 seconds) on average, enabling earlier battery management system intervention. Lead-time analysis at a fixed 0.5 threshold yields a 14.8-frame mean lead time.

</details>


### [123] [Learning-Based Measurement-Robust Control Barrier Functions for Obstacle Avoidance under State Estimation Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20467)
*Nicholas Rober,Yixuan Jia,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种新的控制屏障函数（CBF）方法——DMR-CBF和NMR-CBF，用于在状态估计存在误差的情况下增强安全关键系统中的障碍规避能力。DMR-CBF通过优化漂移动态中的最坏不确定性来提高鲁棒性，而NMR-CBF则用学习项替代该优化，并通过可微轨迹 rollout 进行微调，在保证安全性的同时降低了保守性和计算成本。实验验证了其在仿真和真实四足机器人上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全过滤方法大多假设具有完美状态信息，但在实际中状态估计常存在误差，导致安全违规或过度保守行为。为此，需要更鲁棒的方法来处理估计误差，尤其是在障碍规避等关键任务中。

Method: 提出两种新方法：DMR-CBF 在标准 CBF 条件中引入对漂移动态最坏情况不确定性的内层优化；NMR-CBF 则使用学习机制替代该优化，并通过不同iable轨迹rollout进行微调。DMR-CBF还用于指导NMR-CBF的预训练。

Result: 在平面双积分器和12维四旋翼系统上的数值实验表明，所提方法能在估计误差增大时仍避免碰撞，而其他方法则失败或过于保守；NMR-CBF在保持与DMR-CBF相当的安全性的同时，降低了保守性和计算开销。此外，NMR-CBF成功部署于Unitree Go2机器人，在存在里程计误差的情况下实现了避障导航。

Conclusion: DMR-CBF和NMR-CBF有效提升了在状态估计不准确情况下的系统安全性与实用性，其中NMR-CBF结合学习与优化，在性能、安全性和效率之间取得了良好平衡，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Safety filters are an effective tool for enforcing constraints in safety-critical systems, but most existing methods assume perfect state information, which is rarely available in practice. Recent work has begun to close this gap by developing filtering mechanisms that are robust to state estimation error, but these methods can still exhibit safety violations or overly conservative behavior as estimation error grows. Focusing on obstacle avoidance, we develop two new control barrier function (CBF) formulations: drift-measurement-robust (DMR)-CBFs and neural measurement-robust (NMR)-CBFs. The DMR-CBF augments the standard CBF condition with an inner optimization over the worst-case uncertainty in the drift dynamics, improving robustness to estimation error. This DMR-CBF then supervises a pretraining phase for the NMR-CBF, which replaces the inner optimization with a learned term. The NMR-CBF is subsequently finetuned through differentiable trajectory rollouts, yielding a filter that achieves empirical safety comparable to the DMR-CBF while reducing both conservativeness and computational cost. We provide theoretical analysis of the DMR-CBF along with numerical results on a planar double integrator and a 12D quadrotor, where both proposed approaches prevent collisions while other robust methods either fail or are overly conservative. Finally, we deployed the NMR-CBF on a Unitree Go2, enabling successful navigation of an obstacle field under odometry errors that caused a standard CBF to collide.

</details>


### [124] [AoI-Oriented Globally Optimal Joint Source and Update Scheduling in Fluid Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20545)
*Xiaopeng Yuan,Paul Zheng,Anke Schmeink*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了流体天线在状态更新系统中提升数据新鲜度的潜力，通过联合优化源节点激活、分配及更新周期，最小化最大平均信息年龄（AoI），并提出基于固定点理论的高效算法求解该混合整数非线性问题，验证了流体天线在增强数据新鲜度方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升无线通信中的数据新鲜度，探索流体天线在自适应信道增强和干扰抑制方面的潜力，特别是在多源节点监控同一环境状态的场景下，需联合优化源调度与更新周期。

Method: 采用最大SINR准则进行流体端口选择，建立以最小化最大平均AoI为目标的混合整数非线性优化模型；先固定源调度决策，利用定点理论分析性能可实现性，并设计二分法求解最优更新调度；进一步提出过滤算法剔除非最优调度方案，最终获得全局最优联合解。

Result: 提出了高效的二分法和过滤算法，能够快速收敛到全局最优解；数值结果表明所提方法在不同参数设置下均能显著降低AoI，验证了流体天线对数据新鲜度的提升效果。

Conclusion: 流体天线通过自适应选择端口可有效改善信道增益并抑制干扰，结合联合优化的源调度与更新策略，能显著提升数据新鲜度，为未来智能通信系统提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: As a promising technique, fluid antennas enable adaptive radio environment management and interference mitigation through reconfigurable fluid port selections. In this work, to explore the benefits of fluid antennas for data freshness enhancement, we consider a fluid-antenna assisted status update system supported by multiple source nodes monitoring the same environmental status. We assume a subset of the source nodes are activated to report status updates with different periods. Each user is assigned to one source node and equipped with an fluid antenna to adaptively enhance the channel gain to assigned source node while mitigating interference from other activated source nodes. With maximal signal-to-interference-noise ratio (SINR)-based fluid port selection at all users, we formulate an optimization problem to minimize the maximum average age of information (AoI), where source node activation and assignment, i.e., source scheduling, is jointly optimized with the update periods of all activated source nodes. To optimally solve the resulting mixed-integer nonlinear problem, we first consider given source scheduling decision and apply performance achievability analysis. Aided by fixpoint theory, we propose an efficient bisection algorithm for optimal update scheduling. Based on these characterizations, we further propose a filtering algorithm which efficiently eliminates all non-optimal source scheduling decisions. The globally optimal joint solution is then obtained by combining the resulting optimal source scheduling with its corresponding optimal update scheduling. Numerical results validate the optimality of proposed solution and demonstrate the effectiveness of fluid antennas in enhancing data freshness.

</details>


### [125] [Wireless Power Transfer in Titanium Implants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20550)
*R. W. Porto,L. Murliky,F. R. de Sousa,A. S. de Almeida,H. M. de Albuquerque,V. J. Brusamarello*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了在导电块内嵌入设备的无线能量传输问题，重点分析了涡流对传输效率的影响，并通过钛植入物案例探讨了减少涡流效应的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于涡流会产生反向磁场，显著降低耦合系数和传输效率，因此需要研究如何在导电材料中实现有效的无线能量传输。

Method: 通过建立实验装置，分析接收器嵌入钛植入物时不同抑制涡流方案下电学参数的变化，并设计恒流负载以维持1 mA电流。

Result: 结果显示，若不仔细设计接收器所在腔体的几何结构，该系统可能在技术上不可行。

Conclusion: 在导电块内实现高效的无线能量传输需高度重视接收器腔体的几何设计，以减轻涡流带来的不利影响。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of applications that require wireless power transfer to devices embedded within conductive blocks. The emergence of eddy currents tends to generate a magnetic field opposing that of the transmitter, drastically reducing the coupling coefficient and, consequently, the efficiency of the process. A case study involving a titanium implant is presented and analyzed with a load designed to ensure a constant current of 1 mA. An experimental setup is assembled and evaluated to compare differences in the measured electrical parameters when the receiver is embedded in the implant, considering several solutions that mitigate or avoid the effects of eddy currents. The results show that such a system may be technically unfeasible if careful attention is not paid to the geometry of the cavity in which the receiver is positioned.

</details>


### [126] [A Study on THz Plasmonics in a CMOS Continuum Transistor Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20796)
*Hossein Naghavi,Shruti Chakraborty,Zainulabideen Khalifa,James Gruber,Aditya Varma Muppala,Chen Shen,Denil Sabu,Andreia Cathelin,Ehsan Afshari*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本研究利用28 nm FD-SOI CMOS晶体管中的等离子体波特性，提出一种用于太赫兹（THz）信号放大的新方法，通过连续晶体管阵列（CTA）实现700 GHz频率下的放大验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CMOS在太赫兹频段受限于载流子渡越时间、寄生电容及电流驱动能力下降，难以实现高效信号放大，亟需新型电路设计方法突破频率瓶颈。

Method: 基于等离子体波在连续晶体管阵列（CTA）中的传播机制，结合流体动力学传输模型与Pierce理论，对28 nm FD-SOI CMOS器件中的等离子体波放大进行建模与仿真，并设计制造了工作在700 GHz的门控放大器原型。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在先进工艺节点下可实现太赫兹信号的等离子体波放大；实测证明所设计的700 GHz等离子体波放大器能在传播路径上实现有效增益。

Conclusion: 利用CMOS晶体管沟道中的等离子体效应可突破传统CMOS频率限制，为太赫兹集成电路提供了一种可行的放大器实现方案。

Abstract: This work addresses the limitations of CMOS at terahertz (THz) frequencies, where charge transit time and parasitic capacitances restrict the maximum operating frequency, fmax. As transistor dimensions shrink, reduced current handling capabilities further challenge CMOS, necessitating novel circuit design approaches for the THz domain. By leveraging the plasma characteristics of electron channels in CMOS transistors, this study explores a potential solution for THz signal amplification. Key mechanisms in plasma wave amplification within a continuum transistor array (CTA) formed by 28 nm fully depleted silicon-on-insulator (FD-SOI) CMOS transistors are investigated. A hydrodynamic transport model combined with Pierce's theory is presented to describe plasma wave propagation along the CTA. Simulations of gated amplifiers demonstrate the potential for THz signal amplification in advanced fabrication nodes. Finally, a proof-of-concept plasma wave amplifier operating at 700 GHz has been designed and fabricated, exhibiting amplification along the plasma wave propagation path.

</details>


### [127] [Sharing the Control Authority Between Deep Reinforcement Learning and Model Predictive Control: Application to Multi-Class Transportation Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20858)
*Giray Onur,Azita Dabiri,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合深度强化学习（DRL）和模型预测控制（MPC）的分层框架，用于多车型交通网络控制，在保证实时性的同时提升了控制性能和约束处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如DRL受限于学习能力，MPC依赖精确模型且计算量大，难以在复杂多车型交通网络中实现实时有效控制。

Method: 设计了一种DRL-MPC分层框架：高层MPC负责低频控制输入，利用其优化和约束处理能力；底层DRL负责高频控制输入，发挥其快速在线计算和无需模型的优势。

Result: 在多车型高速公路网络上的实验表明，该框架相比混合状态反馈-MPC控制器性能更优，相较于分层MPC显著降低在线计算时间，并在模型不匹配情况下实现更有效的约束执行。

Conclusion: 所提出的DRL-MPC分层框架有效结合了DRL与MPC的优势，适用于大规模、复杂、存在模型不确定性的多车型交通网络实时控制。

Abstract: Transportation networks, in particular multi-class transportation networks (i.e., networks with mixed vehicle types), are complex systems that are challenging to control. Recently, Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL), which learns control policies from interactions with the environment, and Model Predictive Control (MPC), which uses a system model to optimize control inputs, have been increasingly utilized for transportation network control. However, nonlinear system dynamics and high-dimensional state spaces in large-scale networks limit DRL's learning capacity under time-constrained training and increase MPC's computation time, hindering real-time implementation with limited computational resources. Moreover, MPC depends on an accurate network model, which is often unavailable for complex systems such as multi-class transportation networks. This paper proposes a novel DRL-MPC framework for multi-class transportation networks that divides control authority between DRL and MPC, combining DRL's fast online computation and model independence with MPC's built-in optimization and constraint-handling capabilities. In the hierarchical framework, MPC operates at the higher level and determines low-frequency control inputs whose slower update rate accommodates its high computation time, while DRL operates at the lower level and determines high-frequency control inputs using its fast online deployment. The framework is evaluated on a multi-class freeway network against a hierarchical MPC controller and a hybrid state-feedback-MPC controller, including scenarios with model mismatch and noisy traffic demands. Results show that the proposed framework outperforms the hybrid state-feedback-MPC controller, substantially reduces online computation time compared with the hierarchical MPC controller, and provides more effective constraint enforcement under model mismatch.

</details>


### [128] [Parameters Overshadowed by Price Lags: Load, Climate, and Calendar Effects in ERCOT Day-Ahead Price Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20865)
*Yunus Bicen,Eman Hammad*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种基于XAI的电价预测框架，揭示ERCOT市场中物理驱动因素（如负荷、气候和日历变量）在价格尖峰中的关键作用，发现传统滞后模型会掩盖真实驱动机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电价预测模型过度依赖历史价格滞后项，忽视了负荷、气候等物理驱动因素对市场波动的影响，难以准确捕捉极端价格动态。

Method: 采用直方图梯度提升回归器（HGBR），结合提出的抑制比（Suppression Ratio）量化滞后项对物理变量影响的遮蔽效应，并利用可解释人工智能（XAI）方法分析各特征在不同市场状态下的贡献。

Result: 发现滞后项在正常条件下提供短期记忆，但在价格尖峰期间反而增加误差（MAE/RMSE上升）；真正的价格尖峰源于系统在特定温度区间内的剧烈响应，而非温度持续上升本身。

Conclusion: 应超越传统的滞后依赖模型，重视物理机制与 regime-sensitive 的建模方式，以更准确地识别和预测极端电价事件。

Abstract: Accurate electricity price forecasting is critical for smart grid stability, yet the heavy reliance on historical price lags in modern predictive models often obscures the fundamental physical drivers of market volatility. This paper proposes a regime-sensitive, explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) framework to unmask the hidden roles of load, climate, and calendar variables in the ERCOT Day-Ahead Market (2014-2024). Utilizing a Histogram-based Gradient Boosting Regressor (HGBR), we introduce a Suppression Ratio to quantify how price lags overshadow physical parameters. Our analysis reveals that while lags provide short-term memory during normal conditions, they fail to capture extreme dynamics; notably, their inclusion increased forecasting error (MAE/RMSE) during price spikes. By isolating these effects, the study revealed that price spikes are not merely the result of continuously rising temperatures, but rather emerge from the system reacting sharply within a specific temperature range. These findings underscore the importance of moving beyond lag-based models to uncover the true drivers of extreme price events.

</details>


### [129] [A Safety-Driven Architectural Framework for Fail-Operational Drone Swarms in Critical Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20906)
*Luiz Giacomossi,Zafer Yigit,Marwan Shakarna,Shoaib Saleemi,Ivan Tomasic,Baran Çurüklü,Håkan Forsberg*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合关键性架构框架，用于无人机群在安全关键操作中的认证，通过硬件隔离的安全监控器实现运行时保障，确保非确定性 swarm 算法不影响飞行关键系统，并利用健康向量和马尔可夫模型验证其可靠性可达10^-7/飞行小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机群在安全关键任务中的应用需要可验证的设计保证，但现有的多智能体协调算法具有非确定性，难以满足适航标准对确定性可靠性的要求，因此需要一种能够桥接二者差距的架构解决方案。

Method: 采用SAE ARP4754B方法设计混合关键性架构：1）硬件隔离的安全监控器作为运行时保障网关；2）基于功能危害分析（FHA）导出的智能体健康向量建立形式化安全合约；3）将健康向量传递给集体规划器以实现故障下任务动态重分配；并通过马尔可夫可靠性建模评估系统失效概率。

Result: 该框架能够在不影响飞行关键系统隔离的前提下支持智能 swarm 行为，马尔可夫模型显示，在安全监控器可靠性高于0.9991（符合DAL B等级）时，SAIL IV场景下的危险失效目标（10^-7/飞行小时）理论上可达成。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合关键性架构有效解决了非确定性 swarm 算法与安全关键系统之间的矛盾，为无人机群的适航认证提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: The certification of Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) swarms for safety-critical operations requires verifiable design assurance. Airworthiness standards demand deterministic reliability, whereas multi-agent coordination algorithms execute non-deterministic models. This paper proposes a mixed-criticality architectural framework that applies SAE ARP4754B methods to swarm reconfiguration. First, a hardware-isolated Safety Monitor functions as a Run-Time Assurance (RTA) gateway, decoupling the flight-critical core from the non-deterministic Swarm Manager. Second, the monitor enforces formal safety contracts based on agent Health Vectors derived systematically from a Functional Hazard Assessment (FHA). Third, the framework propagates these Health Vectors to the collective planner to trigger fail-operational task reallocation, enabling intelligent swarm behaviors without compromising flight-critical isolation. Markov reliability modeling demonstrates that the $10^{-7}$ failures per flight hour Hazardous target is theoretically achievable for our SAIL IV scenario, provided the Safety Monitor meets $C_{monitor}>0.9991$, consistent with DAL B CMD/MON implementations.

</details>


### [130] [Multi-Objective Deep Reinforcement Learning for Secure and Stable Power System Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20914)
*Ioannis Papadopoulos,Georgios Tsaousoglou,Johanna Vorwerk*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一控制的深度强化学习代理，用于在多目标约束下优化电力系统的运行，兼顾热安全性和关键模式阻尼性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统运行本质上是多目标的，而现有强化学习方法通常只关注单一运行指标，难以应对复杂实际需求。

Method: 采用深度强化学习框架，设计能同时维持热安全性并提升最关键技术模式阻尼的统一控制代理。

Result: 相比仅关注热安全的代理和常规策略，所提代理在阻尼性能上有显著提升，且几乎不违反热安全约束；在小扰动和大扰动下均表现出更快的振荡衰减和更高的临界清除时间。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了多目标运行下的更好平衡，验证了提高关键阻尼对系统稳定性的运行价值。

Abstract: The ongoing energy transition challenges the stable operation of power systems and increases the need for rapid decision-making under uncertainty. While reinforcement learning has emerged as a promising framework for power system control and operation, existing applications typically focus on a single operational criterion, such as thermal security or small-signal stability. However, power system operation is inherently multi-objective and may involve trade-offs between objectives. This paper develops a unified-control deep reinforcement learning agent that maintains thermal security under stochastic load variations while steering the system toward operating points with improved damping of the most critical mode. Compared to a thermal-security-only agent and a business-as-usual policy, the proposed agent achieves a better balance among the operational objectives considered, with notably improved damping and negligible thermal-security violations. Finally, the operational value of increased critical damping is demonstrated under small- and large-signal disturbances, where operating points with higher damping lead to faster oscillation decay and improved critical clearing times.

</details>


### [131] [Robust lane-change intention anticipation under uncertainty based on a recursive Bayesian filtering approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21132)
*Dilara Kılınç,Hendrik Kleikamp,Bruno Viti*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于运动学特征和周围环境观测的高速公路驾驶员变道意图预测方法，采用递归贝叶斯滤波框架，并注重概率校准以提高预测可靠性，在高D数据集上进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测驾驶员变道意图对于提升自动驾驶系统安全性和交互能力至关重要，现有方法在处理观测缺失和预测校准方面存在不足。

Method: 提出基于递归贝叶斯滤波（可解释为隐马尔可夫模型）的方法，设计了两种似然模型以处理缺失观测，并引入概率校准机制以提升预测可靠性。

Result: 在高D数据集上的实验表明，该方法在鲁棒性、时间一致性和校准性方面优于多种基线方法，能有效应对部分观测缺失的情况。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于贝叶斯滤波并结合校准的变道意图预测方法能够提供可靠且稳健的概率预测，适用于实际驾驶场景中的意图识别任务。

Abstract: In this paper we discuss and analyze a method for lane-change intention anticipation of drivers on highways based on kinematic features and surrounding observations. The approach makes use of a recursive Bayesian filtering strategy, which can be interpreted as a hidden Markov model. We present two models for the likelihood term and show how they can handle missing observations. An additional focus of the work lies on calibrating the obtained probabilities in order to obtain reliable predictions. The introduced approach is evaluated in practice on the highD dataset and compared to several other baseline methods. Particular emphasize is put on evaluating the robustness and temporal consistency as well as calibration of the developed algorithm. Extensive numerical experiments allow for a careful performance assessment and detailed discussion of advantages and limitations of the different methodologies.

</details>


### [132] [Loadability Limits Under Periodic Load Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21256)
*Maitraya Avadhut Desai,Gustavo Valverde,Gabriela Hug*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了周期性变化负载（如数据中心）对电力系统静态负载能力极限的影响，指出传统的静态分析无法捕捉到由强迫周期轨道引起的动态不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于数据中心等新型负载具有周期性变化的特点，传统基于静态平衡点的负载能力评估方法不再适用，需要发展新的理论来准确评估系统的稳定性。

Method: 将经典优化方法从平衡点扩展到周期映射的不动点，通过分析单值矩阵的Floquet乘子来确定周期轨道的分岔点。

Result: 发现随着强迫频率的变化，负载能力极限会显著降低，并且失稳机制可能从电压崩溃转变为转子角折叠；在四总线测试系统中，静态裕度从2.5 p.u.降至0.53 p.u.附近。

Conclusion: 静态分析无法反映周期性负载下的真实稳定性特性，必须采用考虑动态行为的方法进行评估。

Abstract: The static loadability limit, defined as the demand at which the equilibrium equations lose their solution, is the standard basis for interconnection screening of large new loads. This letter shows that when part of the demand varies periodically, as for data-center loads, the steady state is a forced periodic orbit whose loadability limit differs from the static one. The classical optimization argument is extended directly from equilibria to fixed points of the period map. At the limit, the monodromy matrix acquires a Floquet multiplier at +1, so the generic instability is a cyclic fold of the orbit rather than a saddle-node of equilibria. The limit is therefore a function of the forcing frequency, which no static computation can capture. Additionally, with the network Jacobian turning singular along the cycle, singularity-induced instability is extended to orbits. On a four-bus test system, a static margin of 2.5 p.u. shrinks to 0.53 p.u. near the swing frequency, the instability mechanism switches from voltage collapse to a rotor-angle fold, and cold starts fail at amplitudes where the orbit still exists. A static analysis reproduces none of these effects.

</details>


### [133] [Fault Diagnosis of Dynamic Systems Under Unknown Operating Conditions: A Condition-Guided Selective Adaptation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21302)
*Jiaming Liu,Zeyi Liu,Hongshuo Zhao,Pengyu Han,Xiao He*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种条件引导的选择性适应方法，用于在未知工况下进行故障诊断，结合离线对抗学习和在线残差响应更新，提升了模型泛化能力和诊断精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于运行条件变化导致的数据分布偏移，现有诊断模型在实际应用中性能下降，难以应对未知工况下的故障诊断挑战。

Method: 在离线阶段采用连续工况对抗学习与渐进训练策略；在线阶段利用残差工况响应识别可靠的无标签样本并动态更新模型。

Result: 在真实齿轮箱和电机数据集上的实验表明，该方法优于现有最先进方法，具有更高的诊断准确率和较低的测试时间开销。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效增强了故障诊断模型在未知工况下的鲁棒性和泛化能力，具备良好的工业应用前景。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis under unknown operating conditions remains challenging for dynamic industrial systems, as the distribution shift caused by changing operating conditions can significantly degrade the performance of diagnostic models in real-world applications. To address the problem, a condition-guided selective adaptation approach is proposed. Specifically, a novel continuous operating-condition adversarial learning strategy with progressive training is developed in the offline stage to enhance the generalization ability of the diagnostic model. During online deployment, residual operating-condition responses are exploited to identify reliable unlabeled samples from streaming data, which are then used to update the diagnostic model. Extensive experiments on real-world gearbox and motor datasets have demonstrated that the proposed framework outperforms state-of-the-art methods in diagnostic accuracy while maintaining relatively low test-time, showing its potential for practical industrial applications.

</details>
